Company brief

SIGDAL MØLLE AS is a private limited company operating in Engroshandel med korn, råtobakk, såvarer og fôrvarer. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 4.1m, annual result NOK -32.4k. Annual accounts available for 2011-2025 (15 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs.
